- **protein-family-profile-generation**: Use when nucleotide (or protein) FASTA genomes must be converted into Pfam protein-family profiles as input for phenotype classification, e.g. Traitar phypat/phypat+PGL workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-family-profile-generation))
- **protein-family-profiling-with-pfam-hmms**: Use when you need Pfam protein family presence/absence profiles for nucleotide or protein FASTAs (draft genomes, metagenome bins, SAGs) to feed phenotype prediction or comparative genotype-phenotype inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-family-profiling-with-pfam-hmms))
- **protein-family-signature-annotation**: Use when candidate IS nucleotide sequences (FASTA) need protein family annotation — e.g. to decide whether an IS encodes a transposase via InterPro (IPR) / PANTHER (PTHR) accessions before reporting it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-family-signature-annotation))
- **protein-fasta-file-formatting**: Use when preparing the -v (viral) and -nv (nonviral) protein FASTA databases required as input to virMine.py, or when rebuilding virMine 2.0 reference databases after a COG or RefSeq release update. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-fasta-file-formatting))
- **protein-fasta-kegg-annotation-mapping**: Use when starting from an annotated protein .fasta file for a bacterial strain with no existing reconstruction and you need a draft GENRE that can be loaded into COBRApy for downstream flux analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/protein-fasta-kegg-annotation-mapping))
- **protein-feature-concatenation-evaluation**: Use when comparing alternative feature representations (e.g., bag-of-words vs word embeddings vs their concatenation) for protein sequence classification with a gradient-boosting classifier and cross-validated AUC as the selection criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-feature-concatenation-evaluation))
- **protein-feature-vector-construction**: Use when starting from raw FASTA protein sequences and you need a fixed-length dipeptide-composition feature matrix (400 dimensions per protein) to feed downstream feature selection or SVM classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-feature-vector-construction))
- **protein-folding**: Study protein folding mechanisms, predict folding pathways, and analyze folding kinetics. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/protein-sequence-analysis/environment/skills/protein-folding))
- **protein-folding-gpu**: AlphaFold 3, ESM3, Boltz, BioNeMo Fold-CP, OpenFold, and high-throughput protein folding pipelines. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/protein-folding-gpu) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **protein-frequency-counting-across-signature-panels**: Use when you have a multi-signature biomarker output (e.g., muSignAl's sign_cvd2.xlsx) and need to identify which constituent proteins appear most often across panels passing a performance cut-off such as AUC &gt; 0.95. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-frequency-counting-across-signature-panels))
- **protein-function**: Assemble real functional annotation for a protein from its sequence and CAZy family — GO terms and InterPro/Pfam domains, EC number and substrate, and physicochemistry (MW, pI, aromaticity, instability, GRAVY, aa composition) plus sequence features (N-glycosylation sequons, N-terminal signal region). Use this skill whenever the task is to functionally annotate or characterize a protein or CAZyme, to get GO/EC/substrate/domain evidence for a FASTA sequence, to map a CAZy family (GH/GT/PL/CE/CBM/AA) to its EC and substrate, or to compute protein physicochemical properties and glycosylation site… ([Xinpeng021001/dbcan4-advanced](https://github.com/Xinpeng021001/dbcan4-advanced/tree/HEAD/skills/protein-function))
- **protein-function-benchmark-eval**: Evaluates protein language models on classification and regression tasks to assess their capability in predicting protein properties, sub-cellular localization, epitope regions, and mutational effects. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Sub-cellular Localization, Membrane Solubility, Epitope Region Prediction, GB1 Mutational Landscape, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy, Spearman's rank correlation coefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/protein-function-benchmark-eval) · ★3)
- **protein-function-prediction**: Predict protein function and properties from amino acid sequence using BioT5. Use this skill when: (1) You have a protein sequence and want to understand its biological function, (2) You need to identify enzyme activity, pathway involvement, or molecular interactions, (3) You want a concise description of protein properties from sequence alone. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/protein-function-prediction) · ★1.1k)
- **protein-function-prediction**: 🔬 Protein Function Prediction - 预测蛋白质的整体功能和性质。当用户需要预测蛋白质溶解性、亚细胞定位、金属离子结合、热稳定性、信号肽或最适温度时使用此技能。支持 FASTA 序列输入。 ([helplessness/benchthread](https://github.com/helplessness/benchthread/tree/HEAD/.tcode/skills/protein-function-prediction) · ★4)
- **protein-function-prediction**: Apply Protein Function Prediction in computational biology and life sciences. Use when analyzing biological data, developing therapeutics, or building bioinformatics pipelines. This skill covers methodologies, tools, and applications in protein function prediction. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/bio/protein-function-prediction) · ★1)
- **protein-gene-mapping-count-projection**: Use when a mosaic integration layout leaves feature entities (e.g., genes or proteins) unconnected by any shared data matrix, so scMoMaT would otherwise receive None placeholders for a modality in some batches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/protein-gene-mapping-count-projection))
- **protein-generative-ai-eval**: This evaluation framework probes the physical validity, functional success, and generalization capability of generative protein models. It emphasizes leakage-aware dataset splits, structural and docking quality metrics, and experimental validation pipelines to ensure designs are biologically plausible and functionally active. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PLINDER, PoseBusters, PDFBench, PoseX, VenusX, FragBench, GeomMotif, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports RMSD. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/protein-generative-ai-eval) · ★3)
- **protein-graph-embedding-eval**: Evaluates the ability of graph neural networks combined with language models to learn structural and sequence representations of proteins. It probes how well the learned embeddings preserve structural similarity via TM-score prediction and generalize to downstream classification tasks across different protein families and out-of-distribution datasets. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Kinase dataset, SCOPe dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MSE. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/protein-graph-embedding-eval) · ★3)
- **protein-guide**: Identifier chains and gates for the protein_* tools - which ID each tool takes (PDB entry, polymer entity, RCSB computed model, AlphaFold entry, UniProt accession or isoform, ligand component), author versus label chains, residue numbering, and reading homolog, ligand, best-available, and PDB-profile results. Use before passing one protein_* result into another call, or reporting scores, counts, chains, or residue numbers from them. ([cyanheads/protein-mcp-server](https://github.com/cyanheads/protein-mcp-server/tree/HEAD/skills/protein-guide) · ★6)
- **protein-guided-nucleotide-alignment-execution**: Use when nucleotide query sequences (SPAdes contigs or raw short reads) must be searched against protein reference sequences (e.g., 957 H. robusta near-USCOs) to recover homologous phylogenetic markers from fragmented genome-skimming data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-guided-nucleotide-alignment-execution))
- **protein-hmm-alignment**: Use when you need to assign Pfam-domain architecture to protein sequences — e.g., aligning representative BGC proteins against biosynthetic domain pHMMs before hierarchical clade clustering or feature-vector construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/protein-hmm-alignment))
- **protein-homology-identification-blastp**: Use when coding genes predicted from an assembled transcriptome are absent from reference annotations (e.g. NCBI/Ensembl GTF) and their identity must be recovered by protein-level homology against a same-species UniProt proteome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-homology-identification-blastp))
- **protein-hydrogen-bond-energy-calculation**: Use when backbone N, C, O, CA coordinates are available (e.g. PDB/mmCIF files like 3PTI, 155C, 2ADK) and donor-acceptor hydrogen bonds must be classified quantitatively by the DSSP energy rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-hydrogen-bond-energy-calculation))
- **protein-identification-count-aggregation**: Use when DIA raw files (.raw/.d/.wiff) have been searched with DIA-NN against a spectral library and you need per-file protein identification counts plus medians aggregated by instrument family (e.g., TripleTOF vs Orbitrap vs timsTOF Pro). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/protein-identification-count-aggregation))
- **protein-identification-quantification-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Protein identification / quantification` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/proteomics/protein-identification-quantification-starter) · ★46)
- **protein-identifier-mapping-uniprot**: Use when a SCANet co-expression module or GRN (e.g. M11 monocyte network or M4 ISC hub network at correlation ≥ 0.8) must be converted to UniProt identifiers before drug–target interaction queries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/protein-identifier-mapping-uniprot))
- **protein-imaging-feature-dynamic-seed-recovery**: Use when Step 1 of DELVE must be reproduced on a preprocessed .h5ad single-cell object (e.g., 2759 cells x 241 imaging-derived features) to identify temporally co-expressed dynamic feature modules and the dynamic seed. Use when the user mentions APOE proteomic signatures, ε2, ε4, or this paper. Only proteins with FDR below 0.05 in the paper's result tables are listed. Checkup labs do not add proteins, and the beta is not a personal effect. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/proteomic-apoe-alzheimers-signatures) · ★1)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/proteomic-benchmark-reproducibility))

- **proteomic-clocks-eye-aging**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. Records ProAge or cProAge predictions a person already has, using the eye-aging proteomic clocks in Yang et al., npj Digital Medicine 2026 (DOI 10.1038/s41746-026-02805-0). Use when the user brings a proteomic age and asks about eye aging, AREDs, ProAge, or cProAge. Acceleration is the residual from regressing chronological age on proteomic age, and those regression coefficients are not printed, so this skill does not compute it. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/proteomic-clocks-eye-aging) · ★1)
